GLEN COVE CREEK AT GLEN COVE NY, USGS site 01302500 (USGS-01302500), stream in Nassau County, New York, draining 14.4 square miles. Daily means from 1938-10-01 to 2010-09-29, a page a water year (October to September), linked below. Discharge in cubic feet a second, gage height in feet above the gauge's datum. Its annual peaks are its floods; its field measurements are the visits that check the gauge. Revisions are USGS's own notes of the site's published history, as written.
